(229)

Then the sum over
n_{1}
can be finished:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=

\displaystyle\ \mathrm{i}2^{-1/2-2n_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\pi e^{-\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\coth(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)(-r_{1})^{n_{3}}x^{2n_{2}-n_{3}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
1+n_{2},1+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\biggr]

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{{}_{3}\mathcal{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{5}\,}{4}$}-n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\end{matrix}\bigg|1\biggr]

\displaystyle-2\times{}_{3}\mathcal{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}-n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{7}\,}{4}$}-n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\end{matrix}\bigg|1\biggr]\bigg\}.

(230)

Thereafter, we use the formula (
C
) again and get

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=

\displaystyle\ \sum_{n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{3}=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\pi^{3}2^{1-n_{3}}e^{-\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathrm{csch}(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)(-r_{1})^{n_{3}}x^{2n_{2}-n_{3}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}-2n_{2}+n_{3}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
1+n_{2},1+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$},1-n_{2}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\biggr].

(231)

The next key step is to devide this result into two parts, based on the parity of the summation index
n_{3}
. When
n_{3}
is odd, we replace
n_{3}
by
2n_{3}+1
and get

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5,\mathrm{odd}}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=

\displaystyle\ \sum_{n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{3}=0}^{\infty}-\mathrm{i}\pi^{3}4^{-n_{3}}e^{-\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathrm{csch}(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)r_{1}^{1+2n_{3}}x^{-1+2n_{2}-2n_{3}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+2n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{2}$}-2n_{2}+2n_{3}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
1+n_{2},1+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{2}$}-n_{2}+n_{3},\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{2}$}-n_{2}+n_{3}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\biggr].

(232)

When
n_{3}
is even, we replace
n_{3}
by
2n_{3}
and get

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5,\mathrm{even}}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=

\displaystyle\ \sum_{n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{3}=n_{2}}^{\infty}\mathrm{i}\pi^{3}2^{1-2n_{3}}e^{-\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathrm{csch}(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)r_{1}^{2n_{3}}x^{2n_{2}-2n_{3}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}-2n_{2}+2n_{3}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
1+n_{2},1+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1-n_{2}+n_{3},1-n_{2}+n_{3}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\biggr].

(233)

Note that the sum over
n_{3}
is not from
0
to
\infty
as some terms vanish because of the factor
\Gamma[1-n_{2}+n_{3}]
in the denominator. This explains our split of
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
into two parts:
Comparing (
D
) with (
D
), we find

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{2}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)+\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5,\mathrm{odd}}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=0.

(234)

Then we only need to compute
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5,\mathrm{even}}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
, which can be directly done:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5,\mathrm{even}}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=\displaystyle\frac{\,{2\mathrm{i}\pi}\,}{1+\coth(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}x^{-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathbf{F}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{-2}(r_{1})\mathbf{F}^{-2}_{-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1}/x).

(235)

Then we get the whole local signal:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{3}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)+\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5,\mathrm{even}}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=2\mathrm{i}\pi\ x^{-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\ \mathbf{F}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{-2}(r_{1})\mathbf{F}^{-2}_{-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1}/x).

(236)

Consequently,

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{LS}}(r_{1},x)=\displaystyle\frac{\,{1-\mathrm{i}\sinh(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\,}{2\pi}r_{1}x^{-1/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathbf{F}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{-2}(r_{1})\mathbf{F}^{-2}_{-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1}/x)+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu).

(237)

Background

The background comes from 5 terms:
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{4}^{\pm\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
,
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{6}^{\pm\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
,
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{7}^{\pm\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
,
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{8}^{\pm\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
, and
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{9}^{\pm\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
. First, for
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{4}^{\pm\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
, one can directly finish the sum over
n_{1}
in (
D
) and get:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{4}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=

\displaystyle\ \sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-2)^{n}\pi^{5/2}e^{-\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\coth(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)x^{1/2+n-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
1+n,\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\end{matrix}\biggr]

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{\mathrm{i}r_{1}\sec\bigl[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{4}$}(1+2n+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\bigr]{}_{3}\mathcal{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}1,1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{7}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{7}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\end{matrix}\bigg|r_{1}^{2}\biggr]

\displaystyle-\csc\bigl[\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi}\,}{4}$}(1+2n+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\bigr]{}_{3}\mathcal{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}1,\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$},1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{5}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{5}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\end{matrix}\bigg|r_{1}^{2}\biggr]\bigg\}.

(238)

We can also deal with
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{6}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
and
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{9}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
in a similar way. Adding these three terms together, we get

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{4}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)+\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{6}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)+\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{9}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)

\displaystyle=\displaystyle\frac{\,{(-1)^{1+n}16\pi^{2}\mathrm{csch}(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\,}{(1+2n)^{2}+4\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}x^{1/2+n-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}1,\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$},1+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{5}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{5}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\end{matrix}\bigg|r_{1}^{2}\biggr].

(239)

For the rest two terms
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{7}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
and
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{8}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
, we apply the same procedure used when simplifying
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{5}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
. Taking
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{7}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
as an example, we expand the
{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}
factor in (
D
) and get

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{7}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=

\displaystyle\ \sum_{n_{1}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{3}=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(-1)^{1+n_{1}+n_{3}}2^{-1/2-2n_{1}+n_{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}}\,}{\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}-2n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\bigl((-1)^{n_{2}}-\mathrm{i}e^{-\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\bigr)\coth(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)

\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{n_{3}}x^{1/2+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\Gamma\biggl[\begin{matrix}-n_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{2}$}+2n_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
1+n_{1},1+n_{2}\end{matrix}\biggr].

(240)

Then the sum over
n_{1}
can be finished:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{7}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{3}=0}^{\infty}2^{-2+n_{2}}\sqrt{\pi}\bigl(\mathrm{i}(-1)^{1+n_{2}}-e^{-\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\bigr)\coth(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\mathrm{csch}(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)(-r_{1})^{n_{3}}x^{1/2+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
1+n_{2}\end{matrix}\biggr]{}_{3}\mathcal{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\bigg|1\biggr].

(241)

One can simplify
\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{8}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}
in the same way and get:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{8}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=\sum_{n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n_{3}=0}^{\infty}2^{-2+n_{2}}\sqrt{\pi}\bigl(e^{-\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}+\mathrm{i}(-1)^{n_{2}}\bigr)\coth(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\mathrm{csch}(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)(-r_{1})^{n_{3}}x^{1/2+n_{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
1+n_{2}\end{matrix}\biggr]{}_{3}\mathcal{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}+\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\bigg|1\biggr].

(242)

After that, we use the transformation of
{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}
in (
C
) again, and find:

\displaystyle{}_{3}\mathcal{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},-\mbox{$\frac{\,{1}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$}\\
\mbox{$\frac{\,{3}\,}{4}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{2}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{n_{3}}\,}{2}$}-\mbox{$\frac{\,{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\,}{2}$},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\bigg|1\biggr]-(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=0,

(243)

which leads to a nontrivial result:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{7}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)+\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{8}^{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(r_{1},x)=0.

(244)

Therefore, the background is totally from the three series in (
D
), and therefore we get:

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{BG},>}(r_{1},x)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{8(-x)^{n}r_{1}}{(1+2n)^{2}+4\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\biggl[\begin{matrix}1,\frac{1}{2}+\frac{n}{2},1+\frac{n}{2}\\
\frac{5}{4}+\frac{n}{2}-\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},\frac{5}{4}+\frac{n}{2}+\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\end{matrix}\bigg|r_{1}^{2}\biggr].

(245)

This completes our computation of line dispersion integral for the 4-point tree seed integral.

Appendix E
Dispersion Integral for a Minkowski One-Loop Correlator

One feature of the dispersive bootstrap is that the UV divergence in the ordinary computation of 1-loop correlators is totally absent. This may be unfamiliar to some readers, so we use a simple example to connect our dispersion method with the more familiar traditional calculation by dimensional regularization.

Figure 12:
The 4-point correlator of four massive scalars
\phi_{1},\cdots,\phi_{4}
with 1-loop bubble exchange of a pair of massive scalar
\sigma
in the
s
-channel.

Our example will be a 4-point 1-loop equal-time correlator of four scalar particles

\phi_{i}
with masses
m_{i}
(
i=1,2,3,4
), mediated by a pair of massive scalar
\sigma
with mass
m
running in a bubble loop, shown in Fig.
12
. We take the two vertices to be
\phi_{1}\phi_{2}\sigma^{2}
and
\phi_{3}\phi_{4}\sigma^{2}
. Then, the diagram in Fig.
12
is computed by the following integral:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}\int_{-\infty}^{0}{\mathrm{d}}t_{1}{\mathrm{d}}t_{2}\,D_{\mathsf{a}}^{(m_{1})}(k_{1};\tau_{1})D_{\mathsf{a}}^{(m_{2})}(k_{2};\tau_{1})D_{\mathsf{b}}^{(m_{3})}(k_{3};\tau_{2})D_{\mathsf{b}}^{(m_{4})}(k_{4};\tau_{2})

\displaystyle\times\int\displaystyle\frac{\,{{\mathrm{d}}^{d}\bm{q}}\,}{(2\pi)^{d}}D^{(m)}_{\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}(q;\tau_{1},\tau_{2})D^{(m)}_{\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}\Big(\big|\bm{k}_{s}-\bm{q}\big|;\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\Big),

(246)

where
D_{\mathsf{a}\mathsf{b}}^{(m)}(k;\tau_{1},\tau_{2})
is the bulk scalar propagator with mass
m
, and is given by:

\displaystyle D_{\pm\mp}^{(m)}(k;t_{1},t_{2})=\displaystyle\frac{\,{e^{\pm\mathrm{i}E(t_{1}-t_{2})}}\,}{2E},

(247)

\displaystyle D_{\pm\pm}^{(m)}(k;t_{1},t_{2})=D_{\mp\pm}^{(m)}(k;t_{1},t_{2})\theta(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})+D_{\pm\mp}^{(m)}(k;t_{1},t_{2})\theta(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}),

(248)

where
E\equiv\sqrt{k^{2}+m^{2}}
.

A computation of this diagram with dimensional regularization has been done in App. F of
[
42
]
. Here we directly quote the result. By setting
d=3-\epsilon
and let
\epsilon\rightarrow 0
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{\text{DR}}=\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{256\pi^{2}E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{1234}}\bigg[\displaystyle\frac{\,{2}\,}{\epsilon}-\gamma_{\text{E}}+\log 4\pi+2

\displaystyle+\displaystyle\frac{\,{2}\,}{E_{12}-E_{34}}\int_{0}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}\xi\,\bigg(E_{34}\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{12}+E_{\text{min}}}\,}{\mu_{R}}-E_{12}\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{34}+E_{\text{min}}}\,}{\mu_{R}}\bigg)\bigg]+\mathcal{O}({\epsilon}),

(249)

where
\xi
is a Feynman parameter,
E_{\text{min}}\equiv\sqrt{k_{s}^{2}+m^{2}/[\xi(1-\xi)]}
,
E_{i}=\sqrt{k_{i}^{2}+m_{i}^{2}}
, and
\mu_{R}
is the renormalization scale. Note that the divergent term
\mathcal{O}({1/\epsilon})
is proportional to
1/(E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{1234})
, and is what we would get by computing a contact diagram with
\phi_{1}\phi_{2}\phi_{3}\phi_{4}
interaction. This is nothing but the local counterterm we should separate from the bare Lagrangian. The finite part of the counterterm is determined by a renormalization condition, and here we can choose the standard
\mkern 2.0mu\overline{\mkern-2.0mu\text{MS}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
scheme, and remove the term proportional to
2/\epsilon-\gamma_{\text{E}}+\log 4\pi
in (
E
) altogether. Then, we get:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{\mkern 2.0mu\overline{\mkern-2.0mu\text{MS}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}=

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{1}\,}{256\pi^{2}E_{1}E_{2}E_{3}E_{4}E_{1234}}\mathcal{I}(E_{12},E_{34},k_{s}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{m}(E_{12},E_{34},k_{s})\equiv

\displaystyle~2+\displaystyle\frac{\,{2}\,}{E_{12}-E_{34}}\int_{0}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}\xi\,\bigg(E_{34}\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{12}+E_{\text{min}}}\,}{\mu_{R}}-E_{12}\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{34}+E_{\text{min}}}\,}{\mu_{R}}\bigg).

(250)

To make things even simpler, we set the loop mass
m=0
, and so that the integral over
\xi
can be done, leading to the following expression:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}(E_{12},E_{34},k_{s})=2+\displaystyle\frac{\,{2}\,}{E_{12}-E_{34}}\bigg(E_{34}\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{12}+k_{s}}\,}{\mu_{R}}-E_{12}\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{34}+k_{s}}\,}{\mu_{R}}\bigg).

(251)

Now, let us consider the dispersion integral for
\mathcal{I}_{0}(E_{12},E_{34},k_{s})
on the complex-
E_{12}
plane, with
E_{34}
and
k_{s}
fixed in the physical region. Clearly the only discontinuity comes from the first logarithmic factor:

\mathop{\text{Disc}}_{E_{12}}\mathcal{I}_{0}(E_{12})=\displaystyle\frac{\,{4\pi\mathrm{i}E_{34}}\,}{E_{12}-E_{34}},~~~~~E_{12}\in(-\infty,-k_{s}).

(252)

Then, we can use this discontinuity to form a dispersion integral. To determine the subtraction order, we note that the full correlator
\mathcal{I}_{0}
approaches to a constant as
E_{12}\rightarrow\infty
:

\lim_{E_{12}\rightarrow\infty}\mathcal{I}_{0}(E_{12})=2-2\log\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{34}+k_{s}}\,}{\mu_{R}}.

(253)

Therefore our dispersion integral should have a first-order subtraction:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}(E_{12})=\mathcal{I}_{0}(0)+\displaystyle\frac{\,{E_{12}}\,}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\int_{-\infty}^{-k_{s}}{\mathrm{d}}E\,\displaystyle\frac{\,{\mathop{\text{Disc}}_{E}\mathcal{I}_{0}(E)}\,}{E(E-E_{12})}.

(254)

This equality can be directly verified by finishing the integral. The lesson to be learned here is that the dispersion integral itself is independent of
\mu_{R}
and is convergent. Similarly, had we started from the discontinuity of the regularized version (
E
) to do the dispersion integral, we will not get any term
\propto 1/\epsilon
. So, the dispersion method here is free of UV regularization procedure; On the other hand, the renormalization-scale dependence cannot be removed. In the dispersion calculation, this dependence is introduced by the subtraction point
\mathcal{I}_{0}(0)
. Clearly, our “ further modified”
\mkern 2.0mu\overline{\mkern-2.0mu\text{MS}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
subtraction corresponds to choosing
\mathcal{I}_{0}(0)=2-2\log(k_{s}/\mu_{R})
.
